1→import { useCallback } from 'react';
     2→import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
     3→import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
     4→import type {
     5→  CreateShotActionInput,
     6→  CreateShotOptions,
     7→  ShotCreationResult,
     8→  UseShotCreationReturn,
     9→} from './shotCreationTypes';
    10→import {
    11→  clearShotSkeletonEvent,
    12→  dispatchShotSkeletonEvent,
    13→} from './shotCreationEffects';
    14→import {
    15→  createEmptyShotPath,
    16→  createShotWithFilesPath,
    17→  createShotWithGenerationPath,
    18→} from './shotCreationPaths';
    19→
    20→export function useCreateShotAction({
    21→  selectedProjectId,
    22→  shots,
    23→  queryClient,
    24→  setIsCreating,
    25→  generateShotName,
    26→  applyPostCreationEffects,
    27→  createShotMutation,
    28→  createShotWithImageMutation,
    29→  handleExternalImageDropMutation,
    30→}: CreateShotActionInput): UseShotCreationReturn['createShot'] {
    31→  return useCallback(async (options: CreateShotOptions = {}): Promise<ShotCreationResult | null> => {
    32→    if (!selectedProjectId) {
    33→      toast.error('No project selected');
    34→      return null;
    35→    }
    36→
    37→    const {
    38→      name,
    39→      generationId,
    40→      generationPreview,
    41→      files,
    42→      aspectRatio,
    43→      dispatchSkeletonEvents = true,
    44→      onProgress,
    45→    } = options;
    46→
    47→    const shotName = name || generateShotName();
    48→    const imageCount = files?.length || (generationId ? 1 : 0);
    49→    if (dispatchSkeletonEvents && imageCount > 0) {
    50→      dispatchShotSkeletonEvent(imageCount);
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    setIsCreating(true);
    54→    try {
    55→      let result: ShotCreationResult;
    56→      if (generationId && !files?.length) {
    57→        result = await createShotWithGenerationPath({
    58→          selectedProjectId,
    59→          shotName,
    60→          generationId,
    61→          generationPreview,
    62→          shots,
    63→          queryClient,
    64→          createShotWithImage: createShotWithImageMutation,
    65→        });
    66→      } else if (files?.length) {
    67→        result = await createShotWithFilesPath({
    68→          selectedProjectId,
    69→          shotName,
    70→          files,
    71→          aspectRatio,
    72→          shots,
    73→          onProgress,
    74→          createShot: createShotMutation,
    75→          uploadToShot: handleExternalImageDropMutation,
    76→        });
    77→      } else {
    78→        result = await createEmptyShotPath({
    79→          selectedProjectId,
    80→          shotName,
    81→          aspectRatio,
    82→          createShot: createShotMutation,
    83→        });
    84→      }
    85→
    86→      applyPostCreationEffects(result, options);
    87→      options.onSuccess?.(result);
    88→      return result;
    89→    } catch (error) {
    90→      if (dispatchSkeletonEvents) {
    91→        clearShotSkeletonEvent();
    92→      }
    93→      normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
    94→        context: 'useShotCreation',
    95→        toastTitle: 'Failed to create shot',
    96→      });
    97→      return null;
    98→    } finally {
    99→      setIsCreating(false);
   100→    }
   101→  }, [
   102→    selectedProjectId,
   103→    generateShotName,
   104→    setIsCreating,
   105→    createShotWithImageMutation,
   106→    shots,
   107→    queryClient,
   108→    createShotMutation,
   109→    handleExternalImageDropMutation,
   110→    applyPostCreationEffects,
   111→  ]);
   112→}
   113→

1→import { mapPathLorasToStrengthRecord } from '../taskCreation';
     2→import { composeOptionalFields, isNonEmptyString } from './taskFieldPolicy';
     3→import { resolveByPrecedence } from './taskParamContract';
     4→import { filterReferenceSettingsByMode } from './referenceImageFiltering';
     5→import type {
     6→  BuildReferenceParamsInput,
     7→  ImageGenerationTaskParams,
     8→  ReferenceMode,
     9→} from './imageGenerationTypes';
    10→
    11→export function buildLorasParam(
    12→  loras: ImageGenerationTaskParams['loras'],
    13→  inSceneLora?: { url: string; strength: number },
    14→): { additional_loras?: Record<string, number> } {
    15→  const lorasMap: Record<string, number> = mapPathLorasToStrengthRecord(loras);
    16→  if (inSceneLora && inSceneLora.strength > 0) {
    17→    lorasMap[inSceneLora.url] = inSceneLora.strength;
    18→  }
    19→  return Object.keys(lorasMap).length > 0 ? { additional_loras: lorasMap } : {};
    20→}
    21→
    22→export function buildReferenceParams(
    23→  styleReferenceImage: string | undefined,
    24→  mode: ReferenceMode | undefined,
    25→  settings: BuildReferenceParamsInput,
    26→): Record<string, unknown> {
    27→  if (!styleReferenceImage) {
    28→    return {};
    29→  }
    30→
    31→  const filteredSettings = filterReferenceSettingsByMode(mode, {
    32→    style_reference_strength: settings.styleReferenceStrength,
    33→    subject_strength: settings.subjectStrength,
    34→    subject_description: settings.subjectDescription,
    35→    in_this_scene: settings.inThisScene,
    36→    in_this_scene_strength: settings.inThisSceneStrength,
    37→  });
    38→
    39→  return {
    40→    style_reference_image: styleReferenceImage,
    41→    subject_reference_image: settings.subjectReferenceImage || styleReferenceImage,
    42→    ...filteredSettings,
    43→    ...composeOptionalFields([
    44→      {
    45→        [REDACTED],
    46→        value: filteredSettings.in_this_scene_strength,
    47→      },
    48→    ]),
    49→  };
    50→}
    51→
    52→export function buildHiresOverride(
    53→  hiresScale: number | undefined,
    54→  hiresDenoise: number | undefined,
    55→  hiresSteps: number | undefined,
    56→  additionalLoras: Record<string, number> | undefined,
    57→  baseLoras: Record<string, number> = {},
    58→): Record<string, unknown> {
    59→  return {
    60→    ...composeOptionalFields([
    61→      { key: 'hires_scale', value: hiresScale },
    62→      { key: 'hires_steps', value: hiresSteps },
    63→      { key: 'hires_denoise', value: hiresDenoise },
    64→    ]),
    65→    ...(additionalLoras && Object.keys(additionalLoras).length > 0
    66→      ? { additional_loras: { ...baseLoras, ...additionalLoras } }
    67→      : {}),
    68→  };
    69→}
    70→
    71→export function buildImageGenerationBaseParams(
    72→  params: ImageGenerationTaskParams,
    73→  taskId: string,
    74→  finalResolution: string,
    75→): Record<string, unknown> {
    76→  const modelName = resolveByPrecedence(params.model_name, 'optimised-t2i') ?? 'optimised-t2i';
    77→  const seed = resolveByPrecedence(params.seed, 11111) ?? 11111;
    78→  const steps = resolveByPrecedence(params.steps, 12) ?? 12;
    79→
    80→  return {
    81→    task_id: taskId,
    82→    model: modelName,
    83→    prompt: params.prompt,
    84→    resolution: finalResolution,
    85→    seed,
    86→    steps,
    87→    add_in_position: false,
    88→    ...composeOptionalFields([
    89→      {
    90→        key: 'negative_prompt',
    91→        value: params.negative_prompt,
    92→        include: isNonEmptyString,
    93→      },
    94→    ]),
    95→  };
    96→}
    97→

+import React, { useState, useCallback, useRef, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
+import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
+import type { ActiveLora, LoraModel } from '@/domains/lora/types/lora';
+import { dedupeActiveLoras, shouldApplyLoraDefaults } from './loraStateHelpers';
+import { useLoraPersistence } from './loraPersistence';
+
+// Re-export types
+export type { ActiveLora } from '@/domains/lora/types/lora';
+export type { LoraModel } from '@/domains/lora/types/lora';
+
+export interface UseLoraManagerOptions {
+  projectId?: string;
+  shotId?: string;
+  selectedLoras?: ActiveLora[];
+  onSelectedLorasChange?: (loras: ActiveLora[]) => void;
+  persistenceScope?: 'project' | 'shot' | 'none';
+  enableProjectPersistence?: boolean;
+  persistenceKey?: string;
+  disableAutoLoad?: boolean;
+  enableTriggerWords?: boolean;
+  onPromptUpdate?: (newPrompt: string) => void;
+  currentPrompt?: string;
+}
+
+export interface UseLoraManagerReturn {
+  selectedLoras: ActiveLora[];
+  setSelectedLoras: (loras: ActiveLora[]) => void;
+  isLoraModalOpen: boolean;
+  setIsLoraModalOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
+  handleAddLora: (lora: LoraModel, isManualAction?: boolean, initialStrength?: number) => void;
+  handleRemoveLora: (loraId: string) => void;
+  handleLoraStrengthChange: (loraId: string, strength: number) => void;
+  hasEverSetLoras: boolean;
+  shouldApplyDefaults: boolean;
+  markAsUserSet: () => void;
+  handleAddTriggerWord?: (triggerWord: string) => void;
+  handleSaveProjectLoras?: () => Promise<void>;
+  handleLoadProjectLoras?: () => Promise<void>;
+  hasSavedLoras?: boolean;
+  isSavingLoras?: boolean;
+  saveSuccess?: boolean;
+  saveFlash?: boolean;
+  renderHeaderActions?: () => React.ReactNode;
+}
+
+const EMPTY_ACTIVE_LORAS: ActiveLora[] = [];
+
+export const useLoraManager = (
+  availableLoras: LoraModel[] = [],
+  options: UseLoraManagerOptions = {},
+): UseLoraManagerReturn => {
+  const {
+    projectId,
+    shotId,
+    selectedLoras: controlledSelectedLoras,
+    onSelectedLorasChange,
+    persistenceScope = 'none',
+    enableProjectPersistence = false,
+    persistenceKey = 'loras',
+    enableTriggerWords = false,
+    onPromptUpdate,
+    currentPrompt = '',
+    disableAutoLoad = false,
+  } = options;
+
+  const isControlledSelection = !!(controlledSelectedLoras && onSelectedLorasChange);
+  const [internalSelectedLoras, setInternalSelectedLoras] = useState<ActiveLora[]>([]);
+  const selectedLoras = useMemo(
+    () => (isControlledSelection
+      ? (controlledSelectedLoras ?? EMPTY_ACTIVE_LORAS)
+      : internalSelectedLoras),
+    [controlledSelectedLoras, internalSelectedLoras, isControlledSelection],
+  );
+
+  const [hasEverSetLoras, setHasEverSetLoras] = useState(false);
+  const [isLoraModalOpen, setIsLoraModalOpen] = useState(false);
+
+  const setSelectedLoras = useCallback((loras: ActiveLora[]) => {
+    if (isControlledSelection) {
+      onSelectedLorasChange?.(loras);
+      return;
+    }
+    setInternalSelectedLoras(loras);
+  }, [isControlledSelection, onSelectedLorasChange]);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (selectedLoras.length <= 1) {
+      return;
+    }
+    const deduped = dedupeActiveLoras(selectedLoras);
+    if (deduped.length !== selectedLoras.length) {
+      setSelectedLoras(deduped);
+    }
+  }, [selectedLoras, setSelectedLoras]);

Now I have enough information to make a thorough assessment. Let me write up my findings:

## Honest Assessment: chore/desloppify-queue-2026-03-02

**Verdict: This branch is NET NEGATIVE despite some legitimate improvements. It's a "bot refactoring gone wrong" situation.**

### The Numbers Don't Lie

- **Commits made:** 78 (a massive refactoring campaign)
- **Files changed:** 1,238 total
- **Net file count:** +199 files (307 created, 108 deleted)
- **Production code net:** ~+5,189 lines (49,641 insertions - 44,452 deletions)
- **But the critical stat:** **5 "fix: revert over-decomposed" commits** that had to surgically undo bot-generated noise

### What Actually Happened

This branch is the output of what appears to be a **desloppification automation tool** that systematically:

1. **Extracted domains correctly** (media-lightbox, lora) - this is the legitimate part
   - Moved ~125 related files from `src/shared/` to `src/domains/`
   - This is architecturally sound
   - BUT then broke 137 tests in the process

2. **Over-decomposed components and hooks** (the bad part)
   - Commit `0d96e3386`: "revert 36 noise type-splits" - bot created 36 micro-files splitting interfaces that were never reused
   - Commit `32c156be5`: "revert 28 more committed type-only interface splits" - another round of trivial splits
   - Commit `3d1700e42`: "revert 13 over-decomposed extractions, inline trivial wrappers" - single-consumer wrapper files that just re-exported
   - Commit `cc2e77c11`: "revert 7 over-decomposed extractions" - inlined hooks/helpers that had zero external reuse

3. **Created massive test breakage**
   - Commit `650deeb9d`: Fixed **137 broken tests** caused by:
     - Broken barrel import deletions (bot removed `index.ts` without updating consumers)
     - JSX syntax errors from overly-aggressive decomposition
     - 98 test files with broken mock patterns
     - Module coverage paths for all relocated files

### The Quality Issues

**Decomposition signal:**
```
useVideoWorkflowBarModel.ts: 100 lines, pure data transformation, single consumer
├─ Custom type wrapper UseVideoWorkflowBarModelInput: unused externally
└─ returns props object that's immediately spread to one component
```
This file should not exist as a separate concern - it's scaffolding without value.

**Hook fragmentation:**
- `shotCreationAction.ts` (112 lines) extracted from consolidated `useShotCreation`
- `imageGenerationBuilders.ts` (97 lines) extracted from consolidated `imageGeneration.ts` 
- These are utilities, not independent concerns. They're now harder to modify together.

**Barrel file proliferation:**
- Created new barrel files in:
  - `src/domains/generation/components/GenerationDetails/index.ts` (1 line, re-exports)
  - `src/shared/components/MediaGallery/index.ts` (3 lines, re-exports)
- Unnecessary navigation overhead for readers

### What Was Genuinely Improved

✓ **Architecture:** media-lightbox and lora now live in proper domains  
✓ **Shared layer cleanup:** Removed dead shims and params extractor wrappers  
✓ **Edge function refactoring:** Unified error handling and auth policy  
✓ **Type narrowing:** Tightened task and JSON boundaries  
✓ **Test coverage:** Added ~70 new test files with real coverage  
✓ **Gallery fixes:** Shot selector click handling fixed  
✓ **Hook consolidation:** Grouped related creation logic (shotCreationAction, shotCreationEffects, etc. - though debate whether this was needed)

### The Troubling Pattern

The commit history shows **active human (or bot) intervention to undo automation mistakes**:
- "fix: repair 137 broken tests"
- "fix: revert 7 over-decomposed extractions"
- "fix: revert 13 over-decomposed extractions, inline trivial wrappers"
- "fix: revert 28 more committed type-only interface splits"
- "fix: revert 36 noise type-splits"

This is a clear **anti-pattern**: you don't spend 5 commits reverting your own decomposition unless it was overshooting. The fact that the developer(s) had to surgically undo ~80 files of bot-generated noise suggests **the refactoring strategy itself was flawed**.

### The Net Assessment

| Category | Rating | Reasoning |
|----------|--------|-----------|
| Architecture | ✓ Good | Domain extraction (media-lightbox, lora) is sound |
| Code quality | ✗ Bad | Added 199 net new files; several are unnecessary wrappers/barrels |
| Decomposition | ✗ Bad | Created single-consumer utilities and type splits, then had to revert them |
| Test health | ✓ Recovered | 137 broken → fixed, but shouldn't have broken them |
| Maintainability | ✗ Mixed | More structure, but also more files to jump through |
| File count creep | ✗ Negative | +199 net files on a "desloppify" branch is backwards |

### Recommendation

**Don't merge this as-is.** The branch makes real improvements (domain extraction, edge function unification) but buries them in ~80 files of noise that had to be reverted. 

Better path forward:
1. Cherry-pick the legitimate commits:
   - Domain extraction (media-lightbox, lora)
   - Edge function refactoring
   - Test additions
   - Bug fixes (gallery click, export mismatches)
2. Discard the decomposition-revert cycle entirely
3. Create a focused PR with net-negative file count, not +199

The branch currently reads as "automated tool created 307 files, engineer had to revert ~150 of them, merge the chaos anyway." That's not desloppification—that's thrashing.

   182→### Task 7: Audit .sort() Without Comparators (6 findings)
   183→**Priority:** Medium-High — `.sort()` on numbers produces wrong results
   184→**Effort:** Small (20 min)
   185→**Score impact:** Resolves up to 6 T3 findings
   186→
   187→**The bug:** `[10, 2, 1].sort()` returns `[1, 10, 2]` because JS `.sort()` converts to strings.
   188→
   189→**Steps:**
   190→1. Run: `desloppify show smells --id sort_no_comparator` (or grep for the finding IDs)
   191→2. For each finding, read the code and determine what's being sorted:
   192→   - **Numbers/dates:** Add comparator `(a, b) => a - b` (or `b - a` for descending)
   193→   - **Strings meant to be case-insensitive:** Add `(a, b) => a.localeCompare(b)`
   194→   - **Strings meant to be case-sensitive:** This is fine, `.sort()` default is correct — wontfix
   195→   - **Objects sorted by a property:** Should already have a comparator — investigate
   196→3. Fix each one with the appropriate comparator
   197→4. Verify build passes

   206→**File:** `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/hooks/useVideoGenerationModalController.ts:36`
   207→
   208→**Current state:** `const clearAllEnhancedPrompts = async () => {};` — empty async stub that's awaited in `generateVideoService.ts` and `useGenerationController.ts`.
   209→
   210→**Steps:**
   211→1. Search the codebase for any "enhanced prompt" storage/clearing logic — is there a real implementation elsewhere?
   212→2. If no real implementation exists: this is vestigial. Remove the function and remove the `await clearAllEnhancedPrompts()` calls from the 2 callsites
   213→3. If a real implementation exists somewhere: wire it up properly
   214→4. Verify build passes
   215→
   216→---
   217→
   218→## Wontfix Categories (with justification)
   219→
   220→### "No direct tests" (271 findings) — WONTFIX
   221→All 271 files have **transitive test coverage** via imports from tested modules. Writing direct unit tests for every file would be massive effort (weeks of work) with marginal return — the code IS tested, just indirectly. Focus test-writing effort on pure logic/utility functions that have complex branching, not on hooks and components that are integration-tested through their consumers.
   222→
   223→### Flat directories (14 findings) — WONTFIX
   224→Moving files into subdirectories doesn't improve code quality. It breaks `git blame`, creates merge conflicts, and makes existing mental models stale. The files in `src/shared/components/` (40 files) and `src/shared/hooks/` are findable via search. Directory restructuring is high-risk, zero-behavior-change work.
   225→
   226→### Magic numbers (29 findings) — WONTFIX
   227→These are domain-specific constants (animation durations, retry counts, buffer sizes, API limits). Extracting `const RETRY_DELAY_MS = 3000` is often LESS clear than `setTimeout(retry, 3000)` in context. The "magic number" rule is for business logic constants that appear in multiple places — these are all single-use.
   228→
   229→### Hardcoded URLs (13 findings) — WONTFIX
   230→API endpoint URLs, documentation links, CDN paths. These are configuration, not code smells. Moving them to a constants file adds indirection without value — the URL is the value, naming it doesn't help.
   231→
   232→### console.error without throw/return (10 findings) — WONTFIX
   233→These ARE the error handling. They log the error and continue execution. This is correct for non-fatal errors in UI code (clipboard failures, analytics failures, etc.). The alternative — throwing — would crash the UI.
   234→
   235→### Dead functions (most of 45 findings) — WONTFIX
   236→Investigated sample: nearly all are **intentional**:
   237→- `logInvalidationEvent`: empty by design, callsites preserved for debugging
   238→- `addCorruptionEvent`: guarded by `__CORRUPTION_TRACE_ENABLED__` flag
   239→- `triggerBrowserDownload`: actually has a real body (desloppify misdetected)
   240→- `transformGeneration`: actually has a real body (desloppify misdetected)
   241→- `noop`: used as default callback (could make props optional, but low-value)
   242→
   243→### Async without await (most of 20 findings) — WONTFIX
   244→Most are functions that return `runTaskCreationPipeline(...)` — they're async because the return type is `Promise<T>`, and the body is `return someAsyncCall(...)`. TypeScript doesn't require `await` before `return` in an `async` function — the promise is forwarded. This is correct code.
   245→
   246→### Subjective review stale (1051 findings) — WONTFIX
   247→"No human has reviewed this file" is metadata tracking, not a code quality issue. Running a holistic review would mark these resolved but wouldn't change any code.
   248→
   249→---

   260→- [ ] Task 7: Audit .sort() without comparators

   262→
   263→**Estimated total effort:** ~5 hours of focused work
   264→**Expected score impact:** 97.1 → ~97.5 (modest — the big win was the 208 dead exports already done)
   265→**Real impact:** Cleaner architecture in the 4 decomposed files, eliminated import cycles, honest dependency graph from barrel inlining
   266→

   331→3. **buildTaskParamsPayload dedup** (issue: 4ad5e4c9) — `src/shared/lib/tasks/segmentTaskPayload.ts`
   332→   - Extract shared optional-field builder for the duplicated key composition
   333→
   334→4. **generationTaskCache getGenerationId bypass** (issue: efffbe4e) — `src/shared/lib/generationTaskCache.ts`
   335→   - Replace inline `media.generation_id || media.id` with `getGenerationId(media)` from mediaTypeHelpers
   336→
   337→---
   338→
   339→## Phase 4: Interface Grouping & Architecture (~4hrs, ~20 issues)
   340→
   341→### Batch 4A: Group wide interfaces into sub-objects
   342→
   343→1. **UseFormSubmissionProps** (22 fields) — `src/shared/components/ImageGenerationForm/hooks/formSubmission/types.ts`
   344→   - Group into: `{ formState, promptConfig, effects }`
   345→   - Update `useFormSubmission.ts` to destructure from groups
   346→
   347→2. **ReferenceActionHandlersInput** (19 fields) — `src/shared/components/ImageGenerationForm/hooks/referenceManagement/types.ts`
   348→   - Group into: `{ identity, referenceState, stateSetters, mutations }`
   349→
   350→3. **ReferenceManagementOutput** (24 fields) — same file
   351→   - Remove raw state setters. Only export handler functions. Halves the API surface.
   352→
   353→4. **PaneControlTab** (18 props) — `src/shared/components/PaneControlTab.tsx`
   354→   - Group into: `{ position, state, handlers, display, dataTour?, actions? }`
   355→
   356→### Batch 4B: Init coupling fixes (issues: 75aab84c, 9b8946ce, 705a061a, 467a6473, a9786da3)
   357→
   358→1. **debugConfig.ts** — Make env reads lazy:
   359→   ```ts
   360→   let _config: DebugConfig | null = null;
   361→   function getDefaultConfig(): DebugConfig { return { /* 10 env reads */ }; }
   362→   export const debugConfig = new Proxy({} as DebugConfig, {
   363→     get(_, prop) { if (!_config) _config = getDefaultConfig(); return _config[prop]; }
   364→   });
   365→   ```
   366→   Extract window attachment to explicit `registerDebugGlobals()` called from `bootstrap.tsx`.
   367→
   368→2. **Edge function SDK clients** — `supabase/functions/ai-prompt/index.ts`, `ai-voice-prompt/index.ts`
   369→   - Move `new Groq(...)` from module scope to lazy getter with error on missing key.
   370→
   371→3. **projectSelectionStore.ts** — Initialize with null, lazy-read on first access.
   372→
   373→4. **settingsWriteQueue.ts** — Throw explicitly if `writeFunction` is null when `enqueueSettingsWrite` is called.
   374→
   375→### Batch 4C: Cross-module boundary fixes (issues: a027edba, 269d0ba2, 93d8cb17)
   376→
   377→1. **LoraModel imports** (20 files via component barrel):
   378→   - Ensure `src/domains/lora/types/lora.ts` exports `LoraModel`
   379→   - Update 20 files to import from `@/domains/lora/types/lora` instead of `@/domains/lora/components/LoraSelectorModal`
   380→   - Keep LoraSelectorModal barrel for the component only
   381→
   382→2. **lightboxOpenState** — Already in correct location (`src/domains/media-lightbox/state/`). The cross-boundary import by `shared/hooks/layout/useBottomOffset.ts` is acceptable — shared needs to know if lightbox is open for layout. **Wontfix.**
   383→
   384→3. **Dead re-export shims** — Already deleted in Phase 1 earlier.
   385→
   386→### Batch 4D: Structure documentation (issues: f3115f6e, 08993759)
   387→
   388→1. Update `structure.md` — Add entries for `src/domains/`, `src/features/`, `src/integrations/`, `src/types/`
   389→2. Move `editVideoDefaults.ts` from `src/shared/settings/config/` to `src/tools/edit-video/settings/` (if not already there)

   392→
   393→## Phase 5: Wontfix & Deferred (~0hrs)
   394→
   395→These get wontfixed with specific justification:
   396→
   397→| Issue | ID | Justification |
   398→|-------|-----|---------------|
   399→| Feature-scale shared modules | 081f0d04 | ShotImageManager/ImageGenerationForm/MediaGallery ARE genuinely shared across tools. Moving 200+ files gains nothing. |
   400→| Media-lightbox mega-domain | 84709769, 3d7e4601 | 232 files, multi-week split project. Organizing hooks into subdirs (Phase 4) is sufficient. |
   401→| Frontend integration tests | ebabd382 | Valid but out-of-scope. Test infrastructure decision, not code quality. |
   402→| LoRA format coexistence | e8d4da87 | Read-side migration. Adding code to force-migrate adds complexity. Formats converge organically. |
   403→| Hardcoded colors (full sweep) | f51e6bda | 109 files, 532 occurrences. Far too large for this effort. Do incrementally per-PR. |
   404→| useSharedLightboxState "triple layer" | 3e2f3be5 | Investigated: does REAL composition (12+ sub-hooks, derived state). Not a pass-through. |
   405→| useEditSettingsPersistence "wrappers" | 014e348d | Investigated: dual-persistence coordination, race-condition protection. Not pass-through. |
   406→| useInlineEditState "forwarding" | b1cb3561, 646ebbb9 | Investigated: orchestrates 9+ sub-hooks, derives ImageEditState. Real composition, not deletable. |
   407→| useShotEditorLayoutPayloadModel | fbaecf94, 98ba9cca | Investigated: explicit field-mapping checklist for wide context provider. Explicitness aids maintenance. |
   408→| LightboxLayout model | 2e3f5fe9 | Investigated: memoization prevents re-renders. 6 context reads + 6 booleans is reasonable. |
   409→| No handler.ts integration test | 8c6e141c | Edge function integration tests require Supabase runtime. Out of scope. |
   410→| No frontend integration tests | ebabd382 | Infrastructure decision, not a code fix. |
   411→| SegmentSlotModeData god interface | d431cbf9 | 30+ fields but represents a genuine cross-boundary contract. Splitting would scatter the API. |
   412→| Directory reorganization | 349f8378, eabc6676, ee2eef1f, b510383b | Moving files into subdirs breaks git blame, creates conflicts. Low value. |
   413→| UI naming inconsistency | d7ecc3b5, 5d43c264 | Renaming directories is high-churn, low-value. |
   414→| Barrel export inconsistency | dd0516f6 | Adding barrels where missing is busywork. |
   415→| Type file naming | 373c3327 | Both patterns (types.ts, Component.types.ts) are fine. |
   416→| Non-hooks in hooks dirs | e2bb50ed | ~30 files. Moving them all breaks blame. Low value. |
   417→| submitSegmentTask in components/ | 503b4ed7 | Single file, works fine where it is. |
   418→| Slot.tsx placement | 825fa01d | Works fine in ui/ root. |
   419→| Mixed API clients in ai-prompt | f84a93f6 | Raw fetch for OpenAI works. Adding SDK dependency isn't clearly better. |
   420→| storagePaths divergence | 4bd84853 | Edge version is actually more robust. Reconciliation is risky. |
   421→| shotImageEntryId deprecation | f37e6fac | 29-file rename. Do incrementally. |
   422→| Cache utils param style | 90e84fbf | Both patterns work. Standardizing is churn. |
   423→| Invalidation param inconsistency | c56f3a99 | 3 sibling functions. Standardizing is churn for 3 callsites. |
   424→| Reference params casing | 770e4529 | Wire format leaks. Mapping function adds complexity. |

### 5. Review Dimensions (20 categories)

The scanning tool evaluates across these dimensions:
1. `naming_quality` - Function/variable names communicating intent
2. `logic_clarity` - Control flow that provably does what it claims
3. `type_safety` - Type annotations matching runtime behavior
4. `contract_coherence` - Functions honoring stated contracts
5. `error_consistency` - Consistent error strategies
6. `abstraction_fitness` - Proper abstraction levels
7. `ai_generated_debt` - AI-generated code quality issues
8. `high_level_elegance` - Architecture elegance
9. `mid_level_elegance` - Module elegance
10. `low_level_elegance` - Function/statement elegance
11. `cross_module_architecture` - Boundary coherence
12. `initialization_coupling` - Module init coupling
13. `convention_outlier` - Deviation from established patterns
14. `dependency_health` - Dependency graph health
15. `test_strategy` - Test coverage and patterns
16. `api_surface_coherence` - API surface consistency
17. `authorization_consistency` - Auth logic consistency
18. `incomplete_migration` - Partially migrated code
19. `package_organization` - File/directory organization
20. `design_coherence` - Overall design patterns

1→import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback, RefObject } from 'react';
     2→
     3→type EventType = 'click' | 'mousedown' | 'touchstart';
     4→
     5→interface UseClickOutsideOptions {
     6→  events?: EventType[];
     7→  enabled?: boolean;
     8→  delay?: number;
     9→  capture?: boolean;
    10→}
    11→
    12→/** Listen for outside clicks and invoke `callback` when they occur. Example: `const ref = useClickOutside(() => setOpen(false));` */
    13→export function useClickOutside<T extends HTMLElement = HTMLDivElement>(
    14→  callback: () => void,
    15→  options: UseClickOutsideOptions = {},
    16→  existingRef?: RefObject<T>
    17→): RefObject<T> {
    18→  const {
    19→    events = ['mousedown'],
    20→    enabled = true,
    21→    delay = 0,
    22→    capture = false,
    23→  } = options;
    24→
    25→  const internalRef = useRef<T>(null);
    26→  const ref = existingRef || internalRef;
    27→  const callbackRef = useRef(callback);
    28→
    29→  useEffect(() => {
    30→    callbackRef.current = callback;
    31→  }, [callback]);
    32→
    33→  const handleEvent = useCallback((event: Event) => {
    34→    if (ref.current && !ref.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
    35→      callbackRef.current();
    36→    }
    37→  }, [ref]);
    38→
    39→  useEffect(() => {
    40→    if (!enabled) return;
    41→
    42→    const subscribe = () => {
    43→      events.forEach((eventType) => {
    44→        document.addEventListener(eventType, handleEvent, { capture });
    45→      });
    46→    };
    47→
    48→    const unsubscribe = () => {
    49→      events.forEach((eventType) => {
    50→        document.removeEventListener(eventType, handleEvent, { capture });
    51→      });
    52→    };
    53→
    54→    const timeoutId = delay > 0 ? setTimeout(subscribe, delay) : null;
    55→    if (!timeoutId) subscribe();
    56→
    57→    return () => {
    58→      if (timeoutId) clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    59→      unsubscribe();
    60→    };
    61→  }, [enabled, events, handleEvent, delay, capture]);
    62→
    63→  return ref;
    64→}
    65→
```

1→import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     2→
     3→export const useStableSkeletonVisibility = (
     4→  isLoading: boolean,
     5→  hideDelay: number = 120
     6→): boolean => {
     7→  const [showStableSkeleton, setShowStableSkeleton] = useState<boolean>(false);
     8→  const hideTimeoutRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
     9→  const lastLoadingStateRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
    10→  
    11→  useEffect(() => {
    12→    if (isLoading !== lastLoadingStateRef.current) {
    13→      lastLoadingStateRef.current = isLoading;
    14→      
    15→      if (isLoading) {
    16→        if (hideTimeoutRef.current) {
    17→          window.clearTimeout(hideTimeoutRef.current);
    18→          hideTimeoutRef.current = null;
    19→        }
    20→        setShowStableSkeleton(true);
    21→      } else {
    22→        hideTimeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
    23→          setShowStableSkeleton(false);
    24→          hideTimeoutRef.current = null;
    25→        }, hideDelay);
    26→      }
    27→    }
    28→    
    29→    return () => {
    30→      if (hideTimeoutRef.current) {
    31→        window.clearTimeout(hideTimeoutRef.current);
    32→        hideTimeoutRef.current = null;
    33→      }
    34→    };
    35→  }, [isLoading, hideDelay]);
    36→
    37→  return showStableSkeleton;
    38→};

1→import { useState, useCallback, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
     2→
     3→export function useTemporaryVisibility(duration = 2000) {
     4→  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);
     5→  const timeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
     6→
     7→  const clearPendingTimeout = useCallback(() => {
     8→    if (timeoutRef.current) {
     9→      clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);
    10→      timeoutRef.current = null;
    11→    }
    12→  }, []);
    13→
    14→  const show = useCallback(() => {
    15→    setIsVisible(true);
    16→    clearPendingTimeout();
    17→    timeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
    18→      setIsVisible(false);
    19→      timeoutRef.current = null;
    20→    }, duration);
    21→  }, [duration, clearPendingTimeout]);
    22→
    23→  const hide = useCallback(() => {
    24→    setIsVisible(false);
    25→    clearPendingTimeout();
    26→  }, [clearPendingTimeout]);
    27→
    28→  useEffect(() => {
    29→    return () => clearPendingTimeout();
    30→  }, [clearPendingTimeout]);
    31→
    32→  return { isVisible, show, hide };
    33→}
    34→
```

1→import { Task } from '@/types/tasks';
     2→import { TASK_NAME_ABBREVIATIONS } from '../constants';
     3→import { getSupabaseClient as supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';
     4→import {
     5→  asRecord,
     6→  asString,
     7→  asStringArray,
     8→  firstString as firstNonEmptyString,
     9→} from '@/shared/lib/jsonNarrowing';
    10→import { deriveInputImages } from '@/shared/lib/taskParamsUtils';
    11→
    12→function firstString(...values: unknown[]): string | null {
    13→  return firstNonEmptyString(...values);
    14→}
    15→
    16→function toNonEmptyStringArray(value: unknown): string[] {
    17→  return (asStringArray(value) ?? []).filter((item) => item.length > 0);
    18→}
    19→
    20→export const deriveTaskInputImages = (task: Task | null): string[] => {
    21→  if (!task?.params) {
    22→    return [];
    23→  }
    24→  const params = asRecord(task.params) ?? {};
    25→  return deriveInputImages(params);
    26→};
    27→
    28→export const getAbbreviatedTaskName = (fullName: string): string => {
    29→  return TASK_NAME_ABBREVIATIONS[fullName] || fullName;
    30→};
    31→
    32→export const parseTaskParamsForDisplay = (params: unknown): { parsed: Record<string, unknown>; promptText: string } => {
    33→  let parsed: Record<string, unknown> = {};
    34→
    35→  try {
    36→    const candidate = typeof params === 'string' ? JSON.parse(params) : params;
    37→    parsed = asRecord(candidate) ?? {};
    38→  } catch {
    39→    parsed = {};
    40→  }
    41→
    42→  const orchestratorDetails = asRecord(parsed.orchestrator_details);
    43→  const promptText = asString(orchestratorDetails?.prompt) ?? asString(parsed.prompt) ?? '';
    44→  return { parsed, promptText };
    45→};
    46→
    47→export const extractShotId = (task: Task): string | null => {
    48→  const params = asRecord(task.params) ?? {};
    49→  const orchestratorDetails = asRecord(params.orchestrator_details);
    50→  const orchestratorPayload = asRecord(params.full_orchestrator_payload);
    51→  const segmentParams = asRecord(params.individual_segment_params);
    52→
    53→  return firstString(
    54→    orchestratorDetails?.shot_id,
    55→    orchestratorPayload?.shot_id,
    56→    segmentParams?.shot_id,
    57→    params.shot_id,
    58→  );
    59→};
    60→
    61→export const extractSourceGenerationId = (params: Record<string, unknown>): string | undefined => {
    62→  return firstString(
    63→    params.based_on,
    64→    params.source_generation_id,
    65→    params.generation_id,
    66→    params.input_generation_id,
    67→    params.parent_generation_id,
    68→  ) ?? undefined;
    69→};
    70→
    71→export const extractTaskParentGenerationId = (params: Record<string, unknown>): string | undefined => {
    72→  const orchestratorDetails = asRecord(params.orchestrator_details);
    73→  const orchestratorPayload = asRecord(params.full_orchestrator_payload);
    74→
    75→  return firstString(
    76→    params.parent_generation_id,
    77→    orchestratorDetails?.parent_generation_id,
    78→    orchestratorPayload?.parent_generation_id,
    79→  ) ?? undefined;
    80→};
    81→
    82→export const extractPairShotGenerationId = (task: Task): string | null => {
    83→  const params = asRecord(task.params) ?? {};
    84→  const segmentParams = asRecord(params.individual_segment_params);
    85→  const orchestratorDetails = asRecord(params.orchestrator_details);
    86→  const pairIds = toNonEmptyStringArray(orchestratorDetails?.pair_shot_generation_ids);
    87→  const segmentIndex = typeof params.segment_index === 'number'
    88→    ? params.segment_index
    89→    : Number(params.segment_index ?? 0);
    90→  const safeSegmentIndex = Number.isInteger(segmentIndex) && segmentIndex >= 0 ? segmentIndex : 0;
    91→
    92→  return firstString(
    93→    params.pair_shot_generation_id,
    94→    segmentParams?.pair_shot_generation_id,
    95→    pairIds[safeSegmentIndex],
    96→  );
    97→};
    98→
    99→export const isSegmentVideoTask = (task: Task): boolean => {
   100→  return task.taskType === 'individual_travel_segment';
   101→};
   102→
   103→export type SegmentConnectionResult =
   104→  | { ok: true; connected: boolean }
   105→  | { ok: false; error: string };
   106→
   107→export const checkSegmentConnection = async (
   108→  pairShotGenerationId: string | null,
   109→  shotId: string
   110→): Promise<SegmentConnectionResult> => {
   111→  if (!pairShotGenerationId) {
   112→    return { ok: true, connected: false };
   113→  }
   114→
   115→  const { data, error } = await supabase().from('shot_generations')
   116→    .select('id, shot_id, timeline_frame')
   117→    .eq('id', pairShotGenerationId)
   118→    .maybeSingle();
   119→
   120→  if (error) {
   121→    return {
   122→      ok: false,
   123→      error: error.message || 'Failed to verify segment connection',
   124→    };
   125→  }
   126→
   127→  return {
   128→    ok: true,
   129→    connected: !!data && data.shot_id === shotId && (data.timeline_frame ?? -1) >= 0,
   130→  };
   131→};
   132→
```

```
1→// Centralized debug configuration to control logging noise
     2→// This allows fine-grained control over different debug categories
     3→
     4→interface DebugConfig {
     5→  // Core performance logging
     6→  reactProfiler: boolean;
     7→  renderLogging: boolean;
     8→  
     9→  // Image loading and progressive loading
    10→  progressiveImage: boolean;
    11→  imageLoading: boolean;
    12→  
    13→  // Component-specific debugging
    14→  shotImageDebug: boolean;
    15→  autoplayDebugger: boolean;
    16→  tasksPaneDebug: boolean;
    17→  galleryPollingDebug: boolean;
    18→  
    19→  // UI and interaction debugging
    20→  dragDebug: boolean;
    21→  skeletonDebug: boolean;
    22→  videoDebug: boolean;
    23→  
    24→  // Realtime and networking
    25→  realtimeDebug: boolean;
    26→  reconnectionDebug: boolean;
    27→  
    28→  // Development helpers
    29→  devMode: boolean;
    30→}
    31→
    32→// Environment-based configuration
    33→const getEnvFlag = (key: string, defaultValue: boolean = false): boolean => {
    34→  try {
    35→    const env = (import.meta as ImportMeta & { env?: Record<string, unknown> }).env;
    36→    const value = env?.[key];
    37→    if (typeof value === 'string') {
    38→      return value === 'true' || value === '1';
    39→    }
    40→  } catch { /* intentionally ignored */ }
    41→  return defaultValue;
    42→};
    43→
    44→// Lazy default configuration - env reads deferred until first access
    45→function getDefaultConfig(): DebugConfig {
    46→  return {
    47→    // Core performance - disable by default (too noisy)
    48→    reactProfiler: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_REACT_PROFILER', false),
    49→    renderLogging: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_RENDER_LOGGING', false),
    50→
    51→    // Image loading - reduce noise significantly
    52→    progressiveImage: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_PROGRESSIVE_IMAGE', false),
    53→    imageLoading: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_IMAGE_LOADING', false),
    54→
    55→    // Component debugging - only enable when specifically needed
    56→    shotImageDebug: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_SHOT_IMAGES', false),
    57→    autoplayDebugger: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_AUTOPLAY', false),
    58→    tasksPaneDebug: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_TASKS_PANE', false),
    59→    galleryPollingDebug: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_GALLERY_POLLING', false),
    60→
    61→    // UI debugging - minimal by default
    62→    dragDebug: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_DRAG', false),
    63→    skeletonDebug: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_SKELETON', false),
    64→    videoDebug: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_VIDEO', false),
    65→
    66→    // Realtime - keep essential debugging only
    67→    realtimeDebug: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_REALTIME', true), // Keep for critical issues
    68→    reconnectionDebug: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_RECONNECTION', true), // Keep for critical issues
    69→
    70→    // Development mode - enables minimal essential debugging
    71→    devMode: getEnvFlag('VITE_DEBUG_DEV_MODE', true),
    72→  };
    73→}
    74→
    75→// Runtime configuration - lazily initialized on first access
    76→let runtimeConfig: DebugConfig | null = null;
    77→
    78→function ensureConfig(): DebugConfig {
    79→  if (!runtimeConfig) {
    80→    runtimeConfig = getDefaultConfig();
    81→  }
    82→  return runtimeConfig;
    83→}
    84→
    85→// Public API for controlling debug output
    86→export const debugConfig = {
    87→  // Check if a specific debug category is enabled
    88→  isEnabled: (category: keyof DebugConfig): boolean => {
    89→    return ensureConfig()[category];
    90→  },
    91→
    92→  // Enable/disable specific categories at runtime
    93→  enable: (category: keyof DebugConfig): void => {
    94→    ensureConfig()[category] = true;
    95→  },
    96→
    97→  disable: (category: keyof DebugConfig): void => {
    98→    ensureConfig()[category] = false;
    99→  },
   100→
   101→  // Bulk operations
   102→  enableAll: (): void => {
   103→    const config = ensureConfig();
   104→    Object.keys(config).forEach(key => {
   105→      config[key as keyof DebugConfig] = true;
   106→    });
   107→  },
   108→
   109→  disableAll: (): void => {
   110→    const config = ensureConfig();
   111→    Object.keys(config).forEach(key => {
   112→      config[key as keyof DebugConfig] = false;
   113→    });
   114→  },
   115→
   116→  // Preset configurations
   117→  setQuietMode: (): void => {
   118→    runtimeConfig = {
   119→      ...ensureConfig(),
   120→      reactProfiler: false,
   121→      renderLogging: false,
   122→      progressiveImage: false,
   123→      imageLoading: false,
   124→      shotImageDebug: false,
   125→      autoplayDebugger: false,
   126→      tasksPaneDebug: false,
   127→      galleryPollingDebug: false,
   128→      dragDebug: false,
   129→      skeletonDebug: false,
   130→      videoDebug: false
   131→    };
   132→  },
   133→
   134→  setDevelopmentMode: (): void => {
   135→    runtimeConfig = {
   136→      ...ensureConfig(),
   137→      reactProfiler: false, // Still too noisy for dev
   138→      renderLogging: true,
   139→      progressiveImage: false, // Still too noisy for dev
   140→      imageLoading: true,
   141→      shotImageDebug: true,
   142→      autoplayDebugger: false,
   143→      tasksPaneDebug: true,
   144→      galleryPollingDebug: false,
   145→      dragDebug: true,
   146→      skeletonDebug: true,
   147→      videoDebug: true
   148→    };
   149→  },
   150→
   151→  // Get current configuration
   152→  getConfig: (): DebugConfig => ({ ...ensureConfig() }),
   153→
   154→  // Status report
   155→  status: (): void => {
   156→    const config = ensureConfig();
   157→    console.group('🔍 [DebugConfig] Current Configuration');
   158→    Object.entries(config).forEach(([key, value]) => {
   159→      console.log(`${value ? '✅' : '❌'} ${key}: ${value}`);
   160→    });
   161→    console.groupEnd();
   162→  }
   163→};
   164→
   165→/**
   166→ * Attach debugConfig to window for runtime control in dev tools console.
   167→ * Call explicitly from bootstrap rather than as a module-scope side-effect.
   168→ */
   169→export function registerDebugGlobals(): void {
   170→  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
   171→    (window as Window & { debugConfig?: typeof debugConfig }).debugConfig = debugConfig;
   172→  }
   173→}
   174→
```

    47→## Tech Stack
    48→
    49→| Layer | Technology | Purpose |
    50→|-------|------------|---------|
    51→| **Frontend** | React + Vite + TypeScript | SPA framework & build tooling |
    52→| **Styling** | TailwindCSS + shadcn-ui | Utility CSS with container queries (`@container`) |
    53→| **Backend** | Supabase (Postgres + Edge Functions) | Database, auth, storage, serverless workers |
    54→
    55→**Dev server:** `http://localhost:2222` • **Package managers:** npm (default) or bun
    56→
    57→---
    58→
    59→## Directory Structure
    60→
    61→| Path | Purpose | Key Files |
    62→|------|---------|-----------|
    63→| `/src/app` | App bootstrap & routing | `main.tsx` → `App.tsx` → `routes.tsx` → `Layout.tsx` |
    64→| `/src/pages` | Top-level pages | `HomePage`, `ShotsPage`, `ArtPage`, `SharePage`, `NotFoundPage`, payment pages |
    65→| `/src/tools` | Feature modules | Each tool: `pages/`, `components/`, `hooks/`, `settings.ts` |
    66→| `/src/domains` | Domain-specific logic (billing, lora, media-lightbox, generation) | Business logic not tied to a single tool |
    67→| `/src/features` | Feature slices (tasks, shots, gallery, settings, etc.) | UI + hooks organized by product feature |
    68→| `/src/integrations` | Third-party integrations | Supabase client, auth, realtime, instrumentation |
    69→| `/src/types` | Shared TypeScript types | `database.ts`, `tasks.ts`, `ai.ts`, `env.ts` |
    70→| `/src/shared` | Shared code | `components/ui/` (shadcn), `hooks/`, `contexts/`, `lib/` |
    71→| `/supabase/functions` | Edge Functions | Task processing, payments, AI integration |
    72→| `/supabase/migrations` | DB migrations | Schema changes (use `db push --linked`) |
    73→| Root | Build configs | `vite.config.ts`, `tailwind.config.ts`, `railway.toml` |
    74→
    75→---
    76→
    77→## Core Concepts
    78→
    79→### Tools
    80→Tools live in `/src/tools/{tool-name}/` following a consistent structure. See [adding_new_tool.md](docs/structure_detail/adding_new_tool.md).
    81→
    82→**Active tools:** Image Generation, Video Travel, Animate Characters, Edit Images, Edit Video, Join Clips, Training Data Helper
    83→
    84→### Shots & Generations
    85→- **Generations** = gallery items (images/videos produced by AI tasks)
    86→- **Shots** = containers that organize generations into a timeline
    87→- **`shot_generations`** = join table with position + metadata (pair prompts, timeline frame)
    88→- Data access: `useShotImages(shotId)` → see [data_fetching.md](docs/structure_detail/data_fetching.md)
    89→
    90→### Settings Resolution
    91→Priority: **shot → project → user → defaults**. See [settings_system.md](docs/structure_detail/settings_system.md).
    92→
    93→### Task System
    94→Async queue for AI workloads: client creates task → DB trigger → worker polls → Edge Function completes.
    95→See [task_worker_lifecycle.md](docs/structure_detail/task_worker_lifecycle.md).
    96→
    97→### Realtime
    98→Smart polling + Supabase realtime subscriptions. Connected = no polling; disconnected = 15s fallback.
    99→See [realtime_system.md](docs/structure_detail/realtime_system.md).
   100→
```
